While prior work has extensively explored model- and system-level scaling, algorithm- and task-level scaling remain largely unaddressed, constraining the full potential of agentic systems. At the algorithm level, allocating additional inference-time computation can enhance workflow capacity but introduces cross-path redundancy: overlapping computations across multiple reasoning branches. At the task level, complex tasks can be decomposed into subproblems and delegated across multiple agents for improved scalability and parallelism. However, existing infrastructures' scheduling is unaware of the existence of multiple agents, missing opportunities to optimize resource allocation.
  We propose Hive, a multi-agent infrastructure that enables algorithm- and task-level scaling. Hive features a description frontend that captures per-agent behavior and supports test-time scaling algorithms. Leveraging this specification, our backend introduces two key mechanisms: Logits Cache that reuses intermediate logits across redundant sampling paths to mitigate cross-path redundancy at the algorithm level, and Agent-Aware Scheduling that efficiently allocates compute and KV-cache resources according to agent contributions at the task level. Experiments show that Logits Cache achieves an average speedup of $1.11\times$-$1.76\times$ for re-sampling, and Agent-Aware Scheduling reduces the hotspot miss rate by $33\%$-$51\%$.

</details>


### [115] [LLM-Guided Strategy Synthesis for Scalable Equality Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17364)
*Chenyun Yin,Youwei Xiao,Yuze Luo,Yuyang Zou,Yun Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EggMind：一个LLM引导的端到端框架，用于合成可重用的等式饱和策略，通过领域特定语言EqSatL和智能搜索技术解决e图爆炸问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等式饱和(EqSat)优化需要领域特定策略设计，目前主要依赖人工，成为自动化e图编译器的障碍。现有规则合成框架虽然能自动推断大量重写规则，但加剧了e图爆炸问题。LLM为自动化策略合成提供了可能，但直接演化后端代码在实践中效果不佳，缺乏可重用策略抽象和可操作的反馈

Method: 提出EggMind框架：1) 引入领域特定语言EqSatL，将EqSat策略表示为显式可检查的工件；2) 设计LLM引导的智能工作流，包含证明派生的重写模式缓存和可处理性指导等新技术，在e图增长时保持合成稳定性；3) 高效搜索高质量策略

Result: 在向量化基准测试中，EggMind显著改善了资源-质量权衡：相比完整EqSat，最终成本降低45.1%，峰值RAM减少69.1%。该方法可有效迁移到基于XLA的张量编译器，并在增强重写空间的逻辑合成案例中展示了实际潜力

Conclusion: EggMind通过将EqSat策略表示为显式工件并利用LLM引导的智能搜索，成功解决了e图爆炸问题，实现了高质量EqSat策略的自动化合成，为e图编译器的自动化提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Equality saturation (EqSat) is a powerful optimization paradigm that compactly represents many equivalent programs in an e-graph and delays commitment until extraction selects a lowest-cost program. Making EqSat effective, therefore, requires not only domain-specific rewrite rules but also domain-specific strategies. Today, much of this strategy design is still manual, making it a major obstacle to automating e-graph-based compilers. Recent rule-synthesis frameworks can automatically infer large rewrite vocabularies from semantic specifications, but they also enlarge the rewrite space and further exacerbate e-graph explosion. Although large language models (LLMs) make automated strategy synthesis plausible, directly evolving backend code remains ineffective in practice. The search lacks reusable strategy abstractions and actionable feedback, and can easily trigger e-graph explosion or converge to poor designs.
  We present EggMind, an LLM-guided, end-to-end framework for synthesizing reusable EqSat strategies. At its core, EggMind introduces a domain-specific language, EqSatL, to represent EqSat strategies as explicit and inspectable artifacts. It then proposes an LLM-guided agentic workflow, equipped with novel techniques including proof-derived rewrite motif caching and tractability guidance, to search efficiently for high-quality strategies while keeping synthesis stable under e-graph growth. Evaluation shows that EggMind substantially improves the resource-quality trade-off on vectorization benchmarks, reducing final cost by 45.1% and peak RAM by 69.1% relative to full EqSat. We further show that the same methodology transfers effectively to an XLA-based tensor compiler, and demonstrate its practical potential in a logic-synthesis case study with augmented rewrite spaces.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Meta-Reasoning: Metacognitive Consolidation for Self-Improving LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17399)
*Ziqing Zhuang,Linhai Zhang,Jiasheng Si,Deyu Zhou,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Metacognitive Consolidation框架，通过将元认知经验从过去的推理事件整合为可重用知识，以改进未来的元推理能力，解决现有元推理方法局限于单个实例的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元推理方法大多是片段式的，专注于在单个实例内执行复杂的元推理流程，但忽略了跨实例可重用元推理技能的积累，导致重复的失败模式和持续的高元认知努力。

Method: 提出Metacognitive Consolidation框架：1) 将实例级问题解决结构化，分为推理、监控和控制三个角色，生成丰富的可追溯元级轨迹；2) 通过分层、多时间尺度的更新机制整合这些轨迹，逐步形成演化的元知识。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在不同基准测试和骨干模型上均获得一致的性能提升，并且随着元认知经验的积累，性能随时间持续改进。

Conclusion: Metacognitive Consolidation框架通过积累和整合跨实例的元认知经验，形成可重用的元知识，有效提升了大型语言模型的元推理能力，为解决现有元推理方法的局限性提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning capabilities, and as existing approaches for enhancing LLM reasoning continue to mature, increasing attention has shifted toward meta-reasoning as a promising direction for further improvement. However, most existing meta-reasoning methods remain episodic: they focus on executing complex meta-reasoning routines within individual instances, but ignore the accumulation of reusable meta-reasoning skills across instances, leading to recurring failure modes and repeatedly high metacognitive effort. In this paper, we introduce Metacognitive Consolidation, a novel framework in which a model consolidates metacognitive experience from past reasoning episodes into reusable knowledge that improves future meta-reasoning. We instantiate this framework by structuring instance-level problem solving into distinct roles for reasoning, monitoring, and control to generate rich, attributable meta-level traces. These traces are then consolidated through a hierarchical, multi-timescale update mechanism that gradually forms evolving meta-knowledge. Experimental results demonstrate consistent performance gains across benchmarks and backbone models, and show that performance improves as metacognitive experience accumulates over time.

</details>


### [117] [STRIDE: Strategic Iterative Decision-Making for Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17405)
*Wei Chen,Lili Zhao,Zhi Zheng,HuiJun Hou,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STRIDE是一个用于多跳问答的框架，通过分离战略规划、动态控制和基础执行来解决现有方法中的过早实体承诺和逻辑依赖协调问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳问答方法存在两个主要问题：1) 过早承诺于表层实体而非底层推理结构，使问题分解易受词汇歧义影响；2) 忽视推理步骤间的逻辑依赖，导致执行不协调。

Method: 提出STRIDE框架：1) 元规划器构建实体无关的推理骨架，推迟实体基础化；2) 监督器以依赖感知方式协调子问题执行，实现高效并行化和顺序协调；3) 专门执行模块处理基础事实提取和逻辑推理。还提出STRIDE-FT微调框架，使用自生成的执行轨迹。

Result: 实验表明STRIDE实现了鲁棒且准确的推理，STRIDE-FT有效增强了开源大语言模型的性能。

Conclusion: STRIDE通过分离规划、控制和执行解决了多跳问答中的关键问题，其模块化设计和自监督微调方法为复杂推理任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering (MHQA) enables accurate answers to complex queries by retrieving and reasoning over evidence dispersed across multiple documents. Existing MHQA approaches mainly rely on iterative retrieval-augmented generation, which suffer from the following two major issues. 1) Existing methods prematurely commit to surface-level entities rather than underlying reasoning structures, making question decomposition highly vulnerable to lexical ambiguity. 2) Existing methods overlook the logical dependencies among reasoning steps, resulting in uncoordinated execution. To address these issues, we propose STRIDE, a framework that separates strategic planning, dynamic control, and grounded execution. At its core, a Meta-Planner first constructs an entity-agnostic reasoning skeleton to capture the abstract logic of the query, thereby deferring entity grounding until after the reasoning structure is established, which mitigates disambiguation errors caused by premature lexical commitment. A Supervisor then orchestrates sub-question execution in a dependency-aware manner, enabling efficient parallelization where possible and sequential coordination when necessary. By dynamically deciding whether to retrieve new evidence or infer from existing facts, it avoids redundant queries and error propagation, while fusing cross-branch information and reformulating failed queries to enhance robustness. Grounded fact extraction and logical inference are delegated to specialized execution modules, ensuring faithfulness through explicit separation of retrieval and reasoning. We further propose STRIDE-FT, a modular fine-tuning framework that uses self-generated execution trajectories from STRIDE, requiring neither human annotations nor stronger teacher models. Experiments show that STRIDE achieves robust and accurate reasoning, while STRIDE-FT effectively enhances open-source LLMs.

</details>


### [118] [EHRAG: Bridging Semantic Gaps in Lightweight GraphRAG via Hybrid Hypergraph Construction and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17458)
*Yifan Song,Xingjian Tao,Zhicheng Yang,Yihong Luo,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EHRAG是一个轻量级RAG框架，通过构建超图同时捕捉结构和语义关系，采用混合检索机制提升多跳推理能力，在保持线性索引复杂度的同时实现零token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的轻量级RAG方法主要依赖命名实体识别和结构共现关系，但无法捕捉不相交实体之间的潜在语义连接，限制了多跳推理能力。

Method: EHRAG构建包含结构和语义信息的超图：结构超边基于句子级共现和轻量级实体提取，语义超边通过聚类实体文本嵌入构建。采用结构-语义混合扩散检索机制，结合主题感知评分和个性化PageRank精炼来识别最相关的文档。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，EHRAG优于现有最先进的基线方法，同时保持线性索引复杂度和零token消耗的构建成本。

Conclusion: EHRAG通过超图结构和混合检索机制有效解决了传统轻量级GraphRAG方法中语义连接缺失的问题，在保持效率的同时显著提升了检索增强生成的质量。

Abstract: Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) enhances LLMs by structuring corpus into graphs to facilitate multi-hop reasoning. While recent lightweight approaches reduce indexing costs by leveraging Named Entity Recognition (NER), they rely strictly on structural co-occurrence, failing to capture latent semantic connections between disjoint entities. To address this, we propose EHRAG, a lightweight RAG framework that constructs a hypergraph capturing both structure and semantic level relationships, employing a hybrid structural-semantic retrieval mechanism. Specifically, EHRAG constructs structural hyperedges based on sentence-level co-occurrence with lightweight entity extraction and semantic hyperedges by clustering entity text embeddings, ensuring the hypergraph encompasses both structural and semantic information. For retrieval, EHRAG performs a structure-semantic hybrid diffusion with topic-aware scoring and personalized pagerank (PPR) refinement to identify the top-k relevant documents. Experiments on four datasets show that EHRAG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while maintaining linear indexing complexity and zero token consumption for construction. Code is available at https://github.com/yfsong00/EHRAG.

</details>


### [119] [Language models recognize dropout and Gaussian noise applied to their activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17465)
*Damiano Fornasiere,Mirko Bronzi,Spencer Kitts,Alessandro Palmas,Yoshua Bengio,Oliver Richardson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 语言模型能够检测、定位并一定程度上描述对其激活值的扰动（掩码/高斯噪声），表现出对训练正则化技术的感知能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型是否能感知对其内部激活的扰动，特别是区分训练中使用的dropout和推理时可能添加的高斯噪声，探讨模型是否具有"训练意识"信号及其对AI安全的影响

Method: 对Llama、Olmo、Qwen等8B-32B模型，在目标句子激活上应用两种扰动：(a)掩码模拟dropout，(b)添加高斯噪声，然后通过多项选择题测试模型检测、定位和区分扰动类型的能力

Result: 所有测试模型都能轻松检测和定位扰动，通常达到完美准确率；Qwen模型在零样本条件下识别扰动类型的能力随扰动强度增强而提高，且对翻转的上下文标签敏感，表明具有正确的先验偏好

Conclusion: 语言模型能够感知对其激活的扰动，特别是能区分训练相关的dropout和推理噪声，这可能反映了模型对训练过程的某种"意识"，对AI安全具有重要启示

Abstract: We provide evidence that language models can detect, localize and, to a certain degree, verbalize the difference between perturbations applied to their activations. More precisely, we either (a) \emph{mask} activations, simulating \emph{dropout}, or (b) add \emph{Gaussian noise} to them, at a target sentence. We then ask a multiple-choice question such as ``\emph{Which of the previous sentences was perturbed?}'' or ``\emph{Which of the two perturbations was applied?}''.
  We test models from the Llama, Olmo, and Qwen families, with sizes between 8B and 32B, all of which can easily detect and localize the perturbations, often with perfect accuracy. These models can also learn, when taught in context, to distinguish between dropout and Gaussian noise. Notably, \qwenb's \emph{zero-shot} accuracy in identifying which perturbation was applied improves as a function of the perturbation strength and, moreover, decreases if the in-context labels are flipped, suggesting a prior for the correct ones -- even modulo controls.
  Because dropout has been used as a training-regularization technique, while Gaussian noise is sometimes added during inference, we discuss the possibility of a data-agnostic ``training awareness'' signal and the implications for AI safety.
  The code and data are available at \href{https://github.com/saifh-github/llm-dropout-noise-recognition}{link 1} and \href{https://drive.google.com/file/d/1es-Sfw_AH9GficeXgeqpy87rocrZZ_PQ/view}{link 2}, respectively.

</details>


### [120] [SkillGraph: Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Collaboration with Multimodal Graph Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17503)
*Zheng Nie,Ruolin Shen,Xinlei Yu,Bo Yin,Jiangning Zhang,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillGraph提出了一种联合优化视觉多智能体系统中智能体专业能力和通信拓扑的框架，通过多模态图变换器动态预测查询条件化的协作图，并结合技能设计师从失败案例中提炼推理启发式，实现自我演化的多模态技能库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉多智能体系统面临两个耦合问题：1) 通信拓扑在推理前固定，无法根据视觉内容和查询上下文动态调整；2) 智能体推理能力在部署期间保持静态。这两个问题相互强化：僵化的拓扑无法利用更丰富的智能体专业知识，而静态智能体缺乏针对特定查询进行专业化的动机。

Method: 提出SkillGraph联合框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 多模态图变换器(MMGT)，编码视觉标记、指令语义和活动技能嵌入，预测查询条件化的协作图，实现动态、内容感知的信息流；2) 技能设计师，从失败案例中提炼和精炼推理启发式，构建自我演化的多模态技能库。更新后的技能嵌入反馈到MMGT中，使拓扑能够随能力增长而适应。

Result: SkillGraph在四个基准测试、五种常见MAS结构和四个基础模型上实现了一致的性能改进。

Conclusion: SkillGraph通过联合演化智能体专业能力和通信拓扑，有效解决了视觉多智能体系统中拓扑僵化和智能体能力静态的问题，实现了动态、内容感知的协作和持续的能力提升。

Abstract: Scaling vision-language models into Visual Multiagent Systems (VMAS) is hindered by two coupled issues. First, communication topologies are fixed before inference, leaving them blind to visual content and query context; second, agent reasoning abilities remain static during deployment. These issues reinforce each other: a rigid topology fails to leverage richer agent expertise, while static agents lack incentives to specialize for a given query. We address this with SkillGraph, a joint framework that evolves both agent expertise and communication topology. Within this framework, a Multimodal Graph Transformer (MMGT) encodes visual tokens, instruction semantics and active skill embeddings to predict a query-conditioned collaboration graph, replacing hand-crafted routing with dynamic, content-aware information flow. Complementing this, a Skill Designer distills and refines reasoning heuristics from failure cases, constructing a self-evolving multimodal Skill Bank. Crucially, updated skill embeddings are fed back into the MMGT, enabling the topology to adapt alongside capability growth. Experiments show that SkillGraph achieves consistent improvements across four benchmarks, five common MAS structures and four base models. Code is available at https://github.com/niez233/skillgraph.

</details>


### [121] [COSEARCH: Joint Training of Reasoning and Document Ranking via Reinforcement Learning for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17555)
*Hansi Zeng,Liam Collins,Bhuvesh Kumar,Neil Shah,Hamed Zamani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoSearch框架通过联合训练推理智能体和生成式文档排序模型，使用GRPO优化策略，解决了传统智能搜索中检索系统作为固定工具的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能搜索方法（如Search-R1）将检索系统视为固定工具，仅优化推理智能体，而检索组件保持不变。实验表明，固定检索系统与理想检索系统之间存在高达+26.8%的相对F1改进差距，表明检索系统是扩展智能搜索性能的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出CoSearch框架，通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）联合训练多步推理智能体和生成式文档排序模型。引入语义分组策略，通过令牌级相似性聚类子查询，形成有效的优化组而无需额外rollout。设计复合奖励，结合排序质量信号和轨迹级结果反馈，为排序器提供即时和长期学习信号。

Result: 在七个单跳和多跳QA基准测试中，CoSearch相比强基线模型取得了一致的性能改进。消融研究验证了每个设计选择的有效性。结果表明，联合训练推理智能体和检索系统既可行又具有强大的性能表现。

Conclusion: 联合训练推理智能体和检索系统是未来搜索智能体的关键要素。CoSearch框架通过GRPO优化和语义分组策略，有效解决了检索系统作为性能瓶颈的问题，为智能搜索系统的进一步发展提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Agentic search -- the task of training agents that iteratively reason, issue queries, and synthesize retrieved information to answer complex questions -- has achieved remarkable progress through reinforcement learning (RL). However, existing approaches such as Search-R1, treat the retrieval system as a fixed tool, optimizing only the reasoning agent while the retrieval component remains unchanged. A preliminary experiment reveals that the gap between an oracle and a fixed retrieval system reaches up to +26.8% relative F1 improvement across seven QA benchmarks, suggesting that the retrieval system is a key bottleneck in scaling agentic search performance. Motivated by this finding, we propose CoSearch, a framework that jointly trains a multi-step reasoning agent and a generative document ranking model via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). To enable effective GRPO training for the ranker -- whose inputs vary across reasoning trajectories -- we introduce a semantic grouping strategy that clusters sub-queries by token-level similarity, forming valid optimization groups without additional rollouts. We further design a composite reward combining ranking quality signals with trajectory-level outcome feedback, providing the ranker with both immediate and long-term learning signals. Experiments on seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, with ablation studies validating each design choice. Our results show that joint training of the reasoning agent and retrieval system is both feasible and strongly performant, pointing to a key ingredient for future search agents.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Static Snapshots: A Grounded Evaluation Framework for Language Models at the Agentic Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17573)
*Jazmia Henry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出当前LLM评估框架存在四大系统性缺陷，提出GCE框架和ISOPro系统，通过确定性真实验证器消除奖励破解，在消费级硬件上实现3倍准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型评估框架存在四大系统性失效：分布无效性（评估输入不反映真实交互分布）、时间无效性（评估是事后而非训练集成的）、范围无效性（评估单轮输出而非长视野轨迹）、过程无效性（评估输出而非推理过程）。这些缺陷在RLHF中尤为严重，导致奖励破解成为评估设计而非训练病理的可预测后果。

Method: 提出Grounded Continuous Evaluation (GCE)框架，并开发ISOPro系统：1）用确定性真实验证器替代学习型奖励模型，在可验证奖励领域构造性地消除奖励破解；2）采用CPU可更新的LoRA适配器权重，将硬件需求降低一个数量级；3）在资源受限调度领域（六个难度层级）进行验证。

Result: 在资源受限调度任务中：1）仅通过连续评估可见的能力涌现；2）无需研究者干预形成的隐式课程；3）相比零样本基线3倍准确率提升；4）仅使用0.216%可训练参数在消费级硬件上实现。

Conclusion: 当前LLM评估框架存在结构性缺陷，无法有效评估部署的智能体系统。提出的GCE框架和ISOPro系统通过真实验证器和连续评估解决了奖励破解问题，显著降低了硬件门槛，为实际部署的智能体系统评估提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We argue that current evaluation frameworks for large language models (LLMs) suffer from four systematic failures that make them structurally inadequate for assessing deployed, agentic systems: distributional invalidity (evaluation inputs do not reflect real interaction distributions), temporal invalidity (evaluations are post-hoc rather than training-integrated), scope invalidity (evaluations measure single-turn outputs rather than long-horizon trajectories), and process invalidity (evaluations assess outputs rather than reasoning). These failures compound critically in RLHF, where reward models are evaluated under conditions that do not hold during RL training, making reward hacking a predictable consequence of evaluation design rather than a training pathology. We propose the Grounded Continuous Evaluation (GCE) framework and present ISOPro, a simulation-based fine-tuning and evaluation system. ISOPro replaces the learned reward model with a deterministic ground-truth verifier, eliminating reward hacking by construction in verifiable-reward domains, and operates on LoRA adapter weights updatable on CPU, reducing the hardware barrier by an order of magnitude. We validate ISOPro on a resource-constrained scheduling domain with six difficulty tiers, demonstrating capability emergence visible only through continuous evaluation, an implicit curriculum that forms without researcher curation, and a 3x accuracy improvement over zero-shot baselines, all on consumer hardware with 0.216% trainable parameters.

</details>


### [123] [DIRCR: Dual-Inference Rule-Contrastive Reasoning for Solving RAVENs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17584)
*Jiachen Zhang,Chengtai Li,Jianfeng Ren,Linlin Shen,Zheng Lu,Ruibin Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DIRCR模型通过双路径推理模块结合局部行类比推理和全局整体推理，并使用规则对比学习模块增强特征可分性，显著提升RAVEN数据集上的抽象视觉推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法往往只关注全局上下文或局部行关系，未能整合两者，且缺乏中间特征约束，导致规则捕获不完整和表征纠缠，限制了抽象视觉推理的性能。

Method: 提出双路径推理规则对比学习模型：1) 双路径推理模块包含局部路径（行类比推理）和全局路径（整体推理），通过门控注意力机制整合；2) 规则对比学习模块使用伪标签构建正负规则样本，应用对比学习增强特征可分性，促进抽象可迁移规则学习。

Result: 在三个RAVEN数据集上的实验结果表明，DIRCR显著提升了推理的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: DIRCR通过整合局部和全局推理路径，并引入规则对比学习，有效解决了现有方法在抽象视觉推理中的局限性，实现了更完整规则捕获和更好泛化性能。

Abstract: Abstract visual reasoning remains challenging as existing methods often prioritize either global context or local row-wise relations, failing to integrate both, and lack intermediate feature constraints, leading to incomplete rule capture and entangled representations. To address these issues, we propose the Dual-Inference Rule-Contrastive Reasoning (DIRCR) model. Its core component, the Dual-Inference Reasoning Module, combines a local path for row-wise analogical reasoning and a global path for holistic inference, integrated via a gated attention mechanism. Additionally, a Rule-Contrastive Learning Module introduces pseudo-labels to construct positive and negative rule samples, applying contrastive learning to enhance feature separability and promote abstract, transferable rule learning. Experimental results on three RAVEN datasets demonstrate that DIRCR significantly enhances reasoning robustness and generalization. Codes are available at https://github.com/csZack-Zhang/DIRCR.

</details>


### [124] [KnowledgeBerg: Evaluating Systematic Knowledge Coverage and Compositional Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17621)
*Xiao Zhang,Qianru Meng,Yongjian Chen,Yumeng Wang,Johan Bos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowledgeBerg基准测试揭示LLMs在系统性知识枚举和组合集合推理方面的严重缺陷，特别是在知识宽度和推理深度两个维度上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的许多问题看似简单，但实际需要两种能力：(i) 对有限知识宇宙的系统性覆盖，以及(ii) 在该宇宙上的组合集合推理。研究者观察到当前LLMs在这两方面存在严重不足，因此需要建立一个基准来量化评估这些能力。

Method: 研究者引入了KnowledgeBerg基准，包含4,800个多项选择题，源自1,183个枚举种子，涵盖10个领域和17种语言。基准通过两个正交维度形式化挑战：知识宽度（所需宇宙的基数）和推理深度（组合集合操作的数量）。使用代表性开源LLMs进行评估，并进行诊断分析识别失败阶段。

Result: LLMs表现严重受限：在宇宙枚举任务上仅达到5.26-36.88 F1分数，在知识基础推理任务上仅达到16.00-44.19准确率。诊断分析揭示了三个失败阶段：完整性（缺失知识）、意识（未能识别需求）和应用（推理执行错误）。尽管测试时计算和检索增强带来可测量的改进（分别最多4.35和3.78分），但差距仍然显著。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在组织结构化知识和在有限领域上执行组合推理方面存在根本性限制。KnowledgeBerg基准暴露了这些缺陷，并为未来研究提供了评估工具，数据集已在HuggingFace上公开。

Abstract: Many real-world questions appear deceptively simple yet implicitly demand two capabilities: (i) systematic coverage of a bounded knowledge universe and (ii) compositional set-based reasoning over that universe, a phenomenon we term "the tip of the iceberg." We formalize this challenge through two orthogonal dimensions: knowledge width, the cardinality of the required universe, and reasoning depth, the number of compositional set operations. We introduce KnowledgeBerg, a benchmark of 4,800 multiple-choice questions derived from 1,183 enumeration seeds spanning 10 domains and 17 languages, with universes grounded in authoritative sources to ensure reproducibility. Representative open-source LLMs demonstrate severe limitations, achieving only 5.26-36.88 F1 on universe enumeration and 16.00-44.19 accuracy on knowledge-grounded reasoning. Diagnostic analyses reveal three stages of failure: completeness, or missing knowledge; awareness, or failure to identify requirements; and application, or incorrect reasoning execution. This pattern persists across languages and model scales. Although test-time compute and retrieval augmentation yield measurable gains -- up to 4.35 and 3.78 points, respectively -- substantial gaps remain, exposing limitations in how current LLMs organize structured knowledge and execute compositional reasoning over bounded domains. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/2npc/KnowledgeBerg

</details>


### [125] [Toward Reusability of AI Models Using Dynamic Updates of AI Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17626)
*Peter Bajcsy,Walid Keyrouz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种敏捷、数据驱动、基于社区的方法来生成AI模型文档（模型卡片），缩短文档模板更新滞后时间，提高AI模型可重用性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量训练好的AI模型因缺乏文档或文档模板更新滞后于快速变化的可重用性要求而无法重用，需要缩短文档模板更新滞后时间，使AI文档更符合当前最佳实践

Method: 使用Hugging Face仓库中的AI模型和Zero Draft文档模板作为测试数据集，通过目录对齐和词频统计量化模型下载/点赞（重用指标）与文档质量之间的相关性，开发基础设施定期比较文档模板与社区标准实践

Result: 量化了AI模型重用指标与文档质量指标之间的相关性，开发了定期比较文档模板与社区标准实践的基础设施

Conclusion: 提出了一种交付敏捷、数据驱动、基于社区的AI模型文档标准的方法论，能够改善AI模型的可重用性

Abstract: This work addresses the challenge of disseminating reusable artificial intelligence (AI) models accompanied by AI documentation (a.k.a., AI model cards). The work is motivated by the large number of trained AI models that are not reusable due to the lack of (a) AI documentation and (b) the temporal lag between rapidly changing requirements on AI model reusability and those specified in various AI model cards. Our objectives are to shorten the lag time in updating AI model card templates and align AI documentation more closely with current AI best practices.
  Our approach introduces a methodology for delivering agile, data-driven, and community-based AI model cards. We use the Hugging Face (HF) repository of AI models, populated by a subset of the AI research and development community, and the AI consortium-based Zero Draft (ZD) templates for the AI documentation of AI datasets and AI models, as our test datasets. We also address questions about the value of AI documentation for AI reusability.
  Our work quantifies the correlations between AI model downloads/likes (i.e., AI model reuse metrics) from the HF repository and their documentation alignment with the ZD documentation templates using tables of contents and word statistics (i.e., AI documentation quality metrics). Furthermore, our work develops the infrastructure to regularly compare AI documentation templates against community-standard practices derived from millions of uploaded AI models in the Hugging Face repository. The impact of our work lies in introducing a methodology for delivering agile, data-driven, and community-based standards for documenting AI models and improving AI model reuse.

</details>


### [126] [Poly-EPO: Training Exploratory Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17654)
*Ifdita Hasan Orney,Jubayer Ibn Hamid,Shreya S Ramanujam,Shirley Wu,Hengyuan Hu,Noah Goodman,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Poly-EPO：一种通过集合强化学习训练语言模型的方法，鼓励乐观探索并协同探索与利用，提升推理任务的泛化能力和多样性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是从经验中学习的关键，能帮助智能体找到复杂问题的解决方案、泛化到新问题，并通过测试时计算扩展性能。当前语言模型训练方法在探索与利用的协同方面存在不足，需要一种能显式鼓励探索并促进两者协同的框架。

Method: 提出Polychromic Exploratory Policy Optimization (Poly-EPO)框架，核心思想是训练语言模型生成在奖励函数下集体准确且在推理策略上具有探索性的响应集合。首先开发了在任意目标函数下通过集合强化学习优化语言模型的通用方法，通过修改优势计算使标准RL算法适应此设置。然后提出Poly-EPO，用显式协同探索与利用的目标实例化该框架。

Result: 在一系列推理基准测试中，Poly-EPO提高了泛化能力（表现为更高的pass@k覆盖率），保持了模型生成的更大多样性，并能有效随测试时计算扩展性能。

Conclusion: Poly-EPO框架成功地将探索机制整合到语言模型训练中，通过集合强化学习实现了探索与利用的协同，为语言模型在复杂推理任务中的性能提升提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Exploration is a cornerstone of learning from experience: it enables agents to find solutions to complex problems, generalize to novel ones, and scale performance with test-time compute. In this paper, we present a framework for post-training language models (LMs) that explicitly encourages optimistic exploration and promotes a synergy between exploration and exploitation. The central idea is to train the LM to generate sets of responses that are collectively accurate under the reward function and exploratory in their reasoning strategies. We first develop a general recipe for optimizing LMs with set reinforcement learning (set RL) under arbitrary objective functions, showing how standard RL algorithms can be adapted to this setting through a modification to the advantage computation. We then propose Polychromic Exploratory Policy Optimization (Poly-EPO), which instantiates this framework with an objective that explicitly synergizes exploration and exploitation. Across a range of reasoning benchmarks, we show that Poly-EPO improves generalization, as evidenced by higher pass@$k$ coverage, preserves greater diversity in model generations, and effectively scales with test-time compute.

</details>


### [127] [Stratagem: Learning Transferable Reasoning via Trajectory-Modulated Game Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17696)
*Xiachong Feng,Deyi Yin,Xiaocheng Feng,Yi Jiang,Libo Qin,Yangfan Ye,Lei Huang,Weitao Ma,Qiming Li,Yuxuan Gu,Bing Qin,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STRATAGEM提出了一种改进语言模型推理能力的方法，通过选择性强化抽象、领域无关的推理轨迹，并激励适应性推理发展，解决了现有自博弈方法在推理迁移方面的两个根本障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自博弈方法仅依赖终端游戏结果，无法区分可迁移的推理模式与游戏特定启发式方法。游戏为语言模型开发通用推理能力提供了有吸引力的范式，但需要解决领域特定性和上下文静态性这两个推理迁移的根本障碍。

Method: STRATAGEM通过两个核心组件：1) 推理可迁移性系数，选择性强化展示抽象、领域无关推理的轨迹；2) 推理演化奖励，激励适应性推理发展。该方法解决了领域特定性（学习模式锚定在游戏语义中）和上下文静态性（静态游戏环境无法培养渐进式推理）的问题。

Result: 在数学推理、通用推理和代码生成基准测试中显示出显著改进，特别是在需要多步推理的竞赛级数学问题上表现出特别强的增益。消融研究和人工评估证实了两个组件对可迁移推理的贡献。

Conclusion: STRATAGEM通过选择性强化抽象推理轨迹和激励适应性推理发展，有效解决了语言模型在游戏环境中学习时的推理迁移问题，显著提升了模型在多种推理任务上的性能。

Abstract: Games offer a compelling paradigm for developing general reasoning capabilities in language models, as they naturally demand strategic planning, probabilistic inference, and adaptive decision-making. However, existing self-play approaches rely solely on terminal game outcomes, providing no mechanism to distinguish transferable reasoning patterns from game-specific heuristics. We present STRATAGEM, which addresses two fundamental barriers to reasoning transfer: domain specificity, where learned patterns remain anchored in game semantics, and contextual stasis, where static game contexts fail to cultivate progressive reasoning. STRATAGEM selectively reinforces trajectories exhibiting abstract, domain-agnostic reasoning through a Reasoning Transferability Coefficient, while incentivizing adaptive reasoning development via a Reasoning Evolution Reward. Experiments across mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and code generation benchmarks demonstrate substantial improvements, with particularly strong gains on competition-level mathematics where multi-step reasoning is critical. Ablation studies and human evaluation confirm that both components contribute to transferable reasoning.

</details>


### [128] [Evolutionary Negative Module Pruning for Better LoRA Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17753)
*Anda Cao,Zhuo Gou,Yi Wang,Kaixuan Chen,Yu Wang,Can Wang,Mingli Song,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ENMP是一种用于LoRA专家合并的进化负模块剪枝方法，通过识别并排除有害的LoRA层来提升现有合并算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA合并方法假设所有LoRA矩阵都对合并模型有建设性贡献，但实际存在负模块（特定LoRA层）会在合并时降低全局性能，这是当前合并范式的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出ENMP（进化负模块剪枝），这是一种即插即用的LoRA剪枝方法，在合并前定位并排除有害模块。利用进化搜索策略有效导航模块选择的离散、不可微空间，以识别最优剪枝配置。

Result: 广泛评估表明ENMP能持续提升现有合并算法的性能，在语言和视觉领域都实现了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: ENMP通过识别和剪枝负模块解决了LoRA合并的关键瓶颈，为高效多任务部署提供了有效的即插即用解决方案。

Abstract: Merging multiple Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) experts into a single backbone is a promising approach for efficient multi-task deployment. While existing methods strive to alleviate interference via weight interpolation or subspace alignment, they rest upon the implicit assumption that all LoRA matrices contribute constructively to the merged model. In this paper, we uncover a critical bottleneck in current merging paradigms: the existence of $\textit{negative modules}$ -- specific LoRA layers that inherently degrade global performance upon merging. We propose $\textbf{E}$volutionary $\textbf{N}$egative $\textbf{M}$odule $\textbf{P}$runing ($\textbf{ENMP}$), a plug-and-play LoRA pruning method to locate and exclude these detrimental modules prior to merging. By leveraging an evolutionary search strategy, ENMP effectively navigates the discrete, non-differentiable landscape of module selection to identify optimal pruning configurations. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that ENMP consistently boosts the performance of existing merging algorithms, achieving a new state-of-the-art across both language and vision domains. Code is available at https://github.com/CaoAnda/ENMP-LoRAMerging.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [CSF: Black-box Fingerprinting via Compositional Semantics for Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16363)
*Junhoo Lee,Mijin Koo,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CSF方法，首个仅需查询访问的黑盒方法，用于将微调后的文本到图像模型溯源到受保护的血统，无需预部署水印或模型内部访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业文本到图像模型是有价值的资产，通常受限制性许可保护，但只有在能够检测到违规时这些许可才可执行。现有方法需要预部署水印或模型内部访问，这在商业API部署中不可用。

Method: CSF（组合语义指纹）将模型视为语义类别生成器，使用组合性未指定提示进行探测，这些提示在微调下保持稀有。IP所有者具有不对称优势：可在部署后生成新的提示组合，而攻击者必须预测并抑制更广泛的指纹空间。

Result: 在6个模型家族（FLUX、Kandinsky、SD1.5/2.1/3.0/XL）和13个微调变体上，贝叶斯溯源框架实现了可控风险的谱系决策，所有变体满足支配性准则。

Conclusion: CSF为商业API部署中的模型溯源提供了首个有效的黑盒方法，解决了现有技术需要预部署水印或内部访问的限制，为IP保护提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Text-to-image models are commercially valuable assets often distributed under restrictive licenses, but such licenses are enforceable only when violations can be detected. Existing methods require pre-deployment watermarking or internal model access, which are unavailable in commercial API deployments. We present Compositional Semantic Fingerprinting (CSF), the first black-box method for attributing fine-tuned text-to-image models to protected lineages using only query access. CSF treats models as semantic category generators and probes them with compositional underspecified prompts that remain rare under fine-tuning. This gives IP owners an asymmetric advantage: new prompt compositions can be generated after deployment, while attackers must anticipate and suppress a much broader space of fingerprints. Across 6 model families (FLUX, Kandinsky, SD1.5/2.1/3.0/XL) and 13 fine-tuned variants, our Bayesian attribution framework enables controlled-risk lineage decisions, with all variants satisfying the dominance criterion.

</details>


### [130] [Refunded but Rewarded: The Double Dip Attack on Cashback Reward Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16427)
*S M Zia Ur Rashid,Suman Rath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究现金返现奖励计划中的安全漏洞，发现退款处理不当导致奖励滥用攻击，提出奖励引擎状态机模型和完整性约束


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现金返现奖励计划在支付领域具有重要商业价值，但其业务逻辑很少被视为安全关键表面。现有系统在交易退款时对奖励的累积、兑换和调整存在缺陷，可能导致奖励滥用攻击。

Method: 通过六个合法持有的发行商账户进行受控小规模实验，记录生产系统中的实际行为。将奖励引擎形式化为状态机，引入两个完整性不变量（奖励完整性和退款奖励一致性），开发漏洞分类法，并提出防御性伪算法。

Result: 发现六家发行商存在不同级别的漏洞：发行商A的借记卡返现计划在退款后从不调整奖励，允许确定性双重获取攻击；发行商B存在对账单周期时间差漏洞；发行商F存在时间不对称性漏洞；发行商C、D、E实现了稳健的负余额强制执行机制。主要漏洞已通过漏洞赏金计划报告并得到厂商确认。

Conclusion: 现金返现奖励系统的业务逻辑存在严重安全漏洞，需要形式化建模和完整性约束来确保系统安全。提出的防御算法可以填补已识别的漏洞，提升奖励系统的安全性。

Abstract: Cashback reward programs now serve as central instruments in the competitive landscape of cards, digital wallets, and payment platforms. Despite their financial significance, the business logic governing these programs is seldom treated as a security critical surface. In this paper, we study a class of reward abuse attacks that arise from flaws in how reward systems accrue, redeem, and adjust incentives when underlying transactions are reversed through refunds. Using controlled, small scale experiments on six issuer accounts we legitimately hold, we document a spectrum of real world behaviors in production systems. At one extreme, a debit based cashback program (Issuer A) never adjusts rewards when refunded transactions post, enabling a deterministic double dip cashback reward abuse attack. A credit card program (Issuer B) exhibits an analogous reward integrity violation through a statement cycle timing gap that allows reward redemption before the merchant return window closes. At an intermediate tier, a credit card issuer (Issuer F) creates negative reward entries on refunds at statement close but makes rewards redeemable immediately upon settlement, creating a timing asymmetry that allows users to extract reward value before clawback occurs. At the robust end, three credit card issuers (C, D, and E) implement indefinite negative balance enforcement with proportional clawback. We formalize reward engines as state machines, introduce two integrity invariants (Reward Integrity and Refund Reward Consistency), develop a taxonomy of vulnerability classes mapped to CWE and OWASP, and present defensive pseudo algorithms with a semi formal correctness argument that close the identified loopholes. The primary vulnerability (Issuer A) was reported through a private bug bounty program and has been acknowledged by the vendor; good faith disclosure efforts for Issuer B are detailed in Section 8.

</details>


### [131] [TWGuard: A Case Study of LLM Safety Guardrails for Localized Linguistic Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16542)
*Hua-Rong Chu,Kuan-Chun Wang,Yao-Te Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TWGuard模型，针对台湾语言文化背景优化AI安全护栏，相比基础模型F1提升0.289，误报率降低94.9%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全护栏研究缺乏对语言文化背景差异的考量，导致报告性能与实际部署效果存在差距，需要针对特定语言文化背景进行优化

Method: 利用针对本地语言特征定制的数据集优化护栏模型，以台湾语言背景为代表性案例，开发语言背景优化的TWGuard模型

Result: TWGuard相比基础模型F1提升0.289，误报率降低0.037（94.9%减少），显著优于最强基线模型

Conclusion: 为区域社区建立基于自身语言背景的AI安全标准奠定基础，避免接受主导语言强加的边界限制

Abstract: Safety guardrails have become an active area of research in AI safety, aimed at ensuring the appropriate behavior of large language models (LLMs). However, existing research lacks consideration of nuances across linguistic and cultural contexts, resulting in a gap between reported performance and in-the-wild effectiveness. To address this issue, this paper proposes an approach to optimize guardrail models for a designated linguistic context by leveraging a curated dataset tailored to local linguistic characteristics, targeting the Taiwan linguistic context as a representative example of localized deployment challenges. The proposed approach yields TWGuard, a linguistic context-optimized guardrail model that achieves a huge gain (+0.289 in F1) compared to the foundation model and significantly outperforms the strongest baseline in practical use (-0.037 in false positive rate, a 94.9\% reduction). Together, this work lays a foundation for regional communities to establish AI safety standards grounded in their own linguistic contexts, rather than accepting boundaries imposed by dominant languages. The inadequacy of the latter is reconfirmed by our findings.

</details>


### [132] [A Survey on the Security of Long-Term Memory in LLM Agents: Toward Mnemonic Sovereignty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16548)
*Zehao Lin,Chunyu Li,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该调查论文聚焦LLM智能体持久性内存的安全问题，提出了内存生命周期框架和治理原语，强调内存治理质量是未来安全智能体的关键区分因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM安全研究正从数据泄露转向更关键的问题：具有持久性内存的智能体是否会被持续塑造、跨会话投毒、未经授权访问以及在共享组织状态中传播。现有研究较少关注持久性可写内存的认知和治理特性作为独立安全问题的原因。

Method: 基于认知神经科学和记忆哲学，将智能体内存描述为可塑、可重写和社会传播的，建立了围绕六个阶段（写入、存储、检索、执行、共享、遗忘/回滚）和四个安全目标（完整性、机密性、可用性、治理）的内存生命周期框架，并组织相关文献。

Result: 发现文献主要集中在写入和检索时的完整性攻击，而机密性、可用性、存储/遗忘以及良性持久性故障研究较少；没有已发布的架构覆盖所有九个治理原语；使用LLM本身进行内存安全的研究稀疏但至关重要。

Conclusion: 提出"记忆主权"概念——对可写入内容、可读取者、更新授权时机以及可遗忘状态的可靠、可恢复治理，认为未来安全智能体的差异化不仅在于回忆能力，更在于内存治理质量。

Abstract: Research on large language model (LLM) security is shifting from "will the model leak training data" to a more consequential question: can an agent with persistent, long-term memory be continuously shaped, cross-session poisoned, accessed without authorization, and propagated across shared organizational state? Recent surveys cover memory architectures and agent mechanisms, but fewer center the epistemic and governance properties of persistent, writable memory as the reason memory is an independent security problem.
  This survey addresses that gap. Drawing on cognitive neuroscience and the philosophy of memory, we characterize agent memory as malleable, rewritable, and socially propagating, and develop a memory-lifecycle framework organized around six phases -- Write, Store, Retrieve, Execute, Share, Forget/Rollback -- cross-tabulated against four security objectives: integrity, confidentiality, availability, governance. We organize the literature on memory poisoning, extraction, retrieval corruption, control-flow hijacking, cross-agent propagation, rollback, and governance, and situate representative architectures as determinants of which phases are explicitly governable.
  Three findings stand out: the literature concentrates on write- and retrieve-time integrity attacks, while confidentiality, availability, store/forget, and benign-persistence failures remain sparsely studied; no published architecture covers all nine governance primitives we identify; and using LLMs themselves for memory security remains sparse yet essential.
  We unify these under mnemonic sovereignty -- verifiable, recoverable governance over what may be written, who may read, when updates are authorized, and which states may be forgotten -- arguing future secure agents will be differentiated not only by recall capacity, but by memory governance quality.

</details>


### [133] [SafeLM: Unified Privacy-Aware Optimization for Trustworthy Federated Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16606)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad,Uluğ Bayazıt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeLM是一个统一解决LLM四大安全挑战（隐私、安全、虚假信息、对抗鲁棒性）的框架，通过联邦训练、梯度智能化、加密、防御机制和校准解码等技术，在多个基准测试中取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在关键领域部署日益增多，但缺乏对重叠安全挑战的统一处理方法。现有方法往往孤立地解决隐私、安全、虚假信息或对抗鲁棒性问题，而实际部署中这些挑战相互交织，需要综合解决方案。

Method: SafeLM采用多支柱方法：1) 隐私保护：联邦训练结合梯度智能化和Paillier加密；2) 安全性：集成对抗训练时和推理时攻击的防御机制；3) 虚假信息控制：对比性基础化结合校准解码减少幻觉；4) 对抗鲁棒性：对齐感知的二值化聚合，在保持有界重构质量的同时增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在事实性、毒性和成员推理基准测试中，SafeLM达到98.0%的有害内容检测准确率，减少96.9%的通信开销，将梯度反演PSNR从31.7 dB降至15.1 dB。消融研究表明各组件独立贡献，集成后实现了强大的隐私-效用-效率权衡。

Conclusion: SafeLM提供了一个统一的框架，综合解决LLM的四大安全支柱问题，展示了各组件协同工作能实现更好的隐私保护、安全性和效率平衡，为部署可信赖的大型语言模型提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, yet a unified treatment of their overlapping safety challenges remains lacking. We present SafeLM, a framework that jointly addresses four pillars of LLM safety: privacy, security, misinformation, and adversarial robustness. SafeLM combines federated training with gradient smartification and Paillier encryption for privacy, integrates defenses against training and inference-time attacks, employs contrastive grounding with calibrated decoding to reduce hallucinations, and introduces alignment-aware binarized aggregation to enhance robustness while maintaining bounded reconstruction quality. Across benchmarks on factuality, toxicity, and membership inference, SafeLM achieves 98.0% harmful content detection accuracy, reduces communication by 96.9%, and lowers gradient inversion PSNR from 31.7 dB to 15.1 dB. Ablations show that each component contributes independently, whereas their integration yields a strong privacy utility efficiency trade-off for deploying trustworthy LLMs.

</details>


### [134] [Stringology Based Cryptology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16669)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于字符串学的密码学(SBC)概念，将传统字符串处理和模式匹配技术应用于密码输出分析，通过将密码输出解释为符号序列来检测模式重复、子串分布和结构相关性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代密码原语生成大量序列数据（如密钥流、密文块、哈希输出），传统密码评估方法主要依赖统计随机性测试和代数密码分析技术，需要新的分析视角来补充现有方法

Method: 引入基于字符串学的密码学(SBC)概念，将密码输出解释为符号序列，应用字符串学算法检测模式重复、子串分布和结构相关性，具体包括模式频率分析和子串重复度量技术

Result: 实验结果表明SBC分析为密码序列的结构特性提供了补充性见解，能够有效评估流密码生成的密钥流输出，支持结构密码分析和密码评估的未来研究

Conclusion: 基于字符串学的密码学为密码输出分析提供了新的方法论框架，通过字符串处理技术揭示密码序列的结构特性，可作为传统统计和代数方法的有效补充，具有重要的研究价值和应用前景

Abstract: The modern cryptographic primitives are known to generate large volumes of sequential data like keystreams, ciphertext blocks, and hash outputs. Traditional cryptgraphic evaluation methods rely primarily on statistical randomness tests and algebraic cryptanalysis techniques. This paper introduces the concept of Stringology-Based Cryptology (SBC), which applies classical string processing and pattern matching techniques to analyze structural properties of cryptographic outputs. By interpreting cryptographic outputs as symbolic sequences, stringology algorithms can be used to detect pattern recurrence, substring distributions, and structural correlations. In addition, the paper demonstrate how pattern frequency analysis and substring recurrence metrics can be applied to evaluate keystream outputs generated by stream ciphers. Experimental results illustrate that SBC analysis provides complementary insights into structural characteristics of cryptographic sequences and may support future research in structural cryptanalysis and cryptographic evaluation

</details>


### [135] [Surgical Repair of Insecure Code Generation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16697)
*Gustavo Sandoval,Brendan Dolan-Gavitt,Siddharth Garg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文发现大语言模型生成代码时存在格式可靠性差距：模型能正确识别漏洞但生成代码时仍会引入漏洞，原因是安全表示在早期层编码但直到最后层才被激活，通过单层干预可显著减少不安全代码生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能生成生产代码，但经常引入已知的安全漏洞。研究发现这不是知识缺陷问题，因为同一模型能正确识别和解释漏洞，这表明存在格式可靠性差距，需要探究其机制和解决方案

Method: 通过机制分析发现安全表示在模型早期层编码但在计算上保持惰性，直到最后层才被激活。针对单个层设计每个漏洞的引导向量进行干预，在五个模型、三种架构家族和六种漏洞类型上进行验证

Result: 每个漏洞的引导向量能将不安全代码生成减少高达74%，且开销可忽略。这种机制和修复方法在多个模型、架构和漏洞类型上具有普适性，表明不安全代码生成是可解释性问题而非训练伪影

Conclusion: 大语言模型生成不安全代码的根本原因是格式可靠性差距，安全表示编码与激活之间存在延迟。通过针对性的单层干预可有效解决此问题，这表明不安全代码生成是可解释性问题而非训练数据缺陷

Abstract: Large language models write production code, and yet they routinely introduce well-known vulnerabilities. We show that this is not a knowledge deficit: the same models that generate insecure code, correctly identify and explain the vulnerability when asked directly, this is a gap we call the Format-Reliability Gap. Mechanistic analysis reveals the cause: security representations are encoded from the earliest layers but remain computationally inert until the final layer, where format-compliance demands compete with them. Because the failure is localized to a single layer, per-vulnerability steering vectors reduce insecure generation by up to 74% with negligible overhead. The mechanism and the fix generalize across five models, three architecture families, and six vulnerability types, suggesting insecure code generation is an interpretability problem, not a training artifact.

</details>


### [136] [SafeDream: Safety World Model for Proactive Early Jailbreak Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16824)
*Bo Yan,Weikai Lin,Yada Zhu,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SAFEDREAM：一种基于世界模型的轻量级框架，用于多轮越狱攻击的主动早期检测，能够在LLM生成有害内容前1.06-1.20轮发出警报，无需修改模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法存在三个关键限制：需要昂贵的权重修改、独立评估每轮对话而不建模累积安全侵蚀、只能在有害内容生成后检测攻击。多轮越狱攻击通过看似无害的对话轮次逐步侵蚀LLM安全对齐，成功率超过90%。

Method: 提出SAFEDREAM框架，包含三个组件：(1)安全状态世界模型，将LLM隐藏状态编码为紧凑安全表示并预测其跨轮次演化；(2)CUSUM检测，将每轮弱风险信号累积为可靠证据；(3)对比想象，在潜在空间中同时展开攻击和良性未来以在越狱发生前发出早期警报。

Result: 在三个多轮越狱基准测试（XGuard-Train、SafeDialBench、SafeMTData）上对比8个基线方法，SAFEDREAM在所有基准上实现了最佳检测及时性（在合规前1.06-1.20轮），同时保持竞争力的误报率，并在检测质量上优于基线。

Conclusion: SAFEDREAM成功解决了现有防御方法的局限性，通过世界模型框架实现了多轮越狱攻击的主动早期检测，为LLM安全对齐提供了有效的外部防护模块，无需修改模型权重。

Abstract: Multi-turn jailbreak attacks progressively erode LLM safety alignment across seemingly innocuous conversation turns, achieving success rates exceeding 90% against state-of-the-art models. Existing alignment-based and guardrail methods suffer from three key limitations: they require costly weight modification, evaluate each turn independently without modeling cumulative safety erosion, and detect attacks only after harmful content has been generated. To address these limitations, we first formulate the proactive early jailbreak detection problem with a new metric, detection lead, that measures how early an attack can be detected before the LLM complies. We then propose SAFEDREAM, a lightweight world-model-based framework that operates as an external module without modifying the LLM's weights. SAFEDREAM introduces three components: (1) a safety state world model that encodes LLM hidden states into a compact safety representation and predicts how it evolves across turns, (2) CUSUM detection that accumulates weak per-turn risk signals into reliable evidence, and (3) contrastive imagination that simultaneously rolls out attack and benign futures in latent space to issue early alarms before jailbreaks occur. On three multi-turn jailbreak benchmarks (XGuard-Train, SafeDialBench, SafeMTData) against 8 baselines, SAFEDREAM achieves the best detection timeliness across all benchmarks (1.06-1.20 turns before compliance) while maintaining competitive false positive rates and outperforming baselines in detection quality.

</details>


### [137] [ParikkhaChain: Blockchain-Based Result Processing and Privacy-Preserving Academic Record Management for the Complete Examination Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16827)
*Rabib Jahin Ibn Momin,Ahmed Mahir Sultan Rumi,Rezwana Reaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ParikkhaChain是一个基于区块链的现场考试全生命周期管理框架，通过匿名脚本评估、透明审核流程和隐私保护验证，解决传统考试系统中的证书伪造、结果篡改、考官偏见等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学术考试系统存在集中化、不透明的记录管理问题，容易遭受证书伪造、结果篡改、考官偏见等风险，且缺乏透明的重新评估途径。现有的区块链教育应用主要关注事后证书存储或纯在线考试门户，未能覆盖现场考试的完整生命周期。

Method: 提出ParikkhaChain框架，基于以太坊区块链部署四个Solidity智能合约。采用三种核心技术：(1) 通过答案脚本的密码学哈希实现匿名评估，消除身份偏见；(2) 基于不可变链上审计追踪的透明评估和审核工作流；(3) 使用零知识证明和链下存储机制的隐私保护验证。

Result: 成功模拟了100门课程、数百名师生参与的现场考试完整周期，从考试进行到成绩单生成。实验结果表明，系统能够高效管理数百门课程、学生和教师的在线考试，具有高吞吐量、低存储和交易成本。

Conclusion: ParikkhaChain是首个支持物理考试流程、匿名评分和重新评估透明度的区块链框架，通过完整的现场考试生命周期管理，有效解决了传统考试系统的关键缺陷，代码已开源。

Abstract: Academic examination systems worldwide continue to rely on centralised, opaque record-keeping that is often vulnerable to credential forgery, result tampering, examiner bias, and the absence of transparent re-evaluation pathways. Existing blockchain-based approaches in education focus predominantly on post-hoc certificate storage or online-only examination portals, leaving the complete onsite examination lifecycle, from conducting exams through scrutiny, largely unaddressed. This paper proposes ParikkhaChain, a blockchain-based framework that covers the entire examination lifecycle of an onsite examination system with three distinguishing contributions: (i) anonymous script evaluation through cryptographic hashing of answer scripts before examiner access, thereby eliminating identity-based bias; (ii) a transparent evaluation and scrutiny workflow backed by an immutable on-chain audit trail that records every mark submission and grade revision; and (iii) inclusion of privacy-preserving verification using zero-knowledge proofs and off-chain storage mechanisms. The system is architected around four Solidity smart contracts deployed on the Ethereum blockchain. The proposed architecture is the first initiative to our knowledge to support physical examination process, anonymous marking, and re-evaluation transparency. We successfully simulate full exam cycles of an onsite exam to grade-sheet generation using a working prototype on a large scale of 100 courses and hundreds of teachers and students. The experimental results show that the system can manage online examinations of hundreds of courses, students and faculties efficiently with great throughput, low storage, and transaction cost. Our codebase is available in open source form at https://github.com/AhmedRumi/CSE6608-ParikkhaChain

</details>


### [138] [DALC-CT: Dynamic Analysis of Low-Level Code Traces for Constant-Time Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16832)
*Nges Brian Njungle,Edwin P. Kayang,Mishel J. Paul,Michel A. Kinsy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于动态分析和低层执行轨迹的常数时间程序验证方法DALC-CT，通过比较不同输入下的指令序列分布来检测时序侧信道漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序侧信道攻击利用程序执行时间差异泄露敏感信息，密码学实现尤其脆弱。现有验证方法存在局限：形式化方法抽象过度无法捕捉真实执行行为，基于时序测量的方法易受噪声干扰。需要更可靠、轻量级的常数时间程序验证方法。

Method: 提出基于动态分析低层执行轨迹的方法：1) 对给定二进制文件和目标函数，测量多个输入值下的指令序列；2) 比较任意一对轨迹的指令混合分布；3) 任何分布差异都表明违反常数时间原则。开发开源工具DALC-CT实现该方法。

Result: 在已知常数时间和非常数时间示例集上评估DALC-CT，实现了完美的问题检测。结果表明通过指令轨迹比较分析程序逻辑执行，为验证常数时间属性提供了轻量级且可靠的方法。

Conclusion: 基于动态分析和低层执行轨迹比较的方法能有效验证常数时间程序，克服了形式化方法和时序测量的局限性。DALC-CT工具展示了该方法在实际验证中的可行性和可靠性。

Abstract: Timing side-channel attacks exploit variations in program execution time to recover sensitive information. Cryptographic implementations are especially vulnerable to these attacks, since even small timing differences in operations such as modular exponentiation or key comparisons can be exploited to extract highly sensitive information, such as secret keys. To mitigate this threat, implementations of programs that handle sensitive information are often expected to adhere to constant-time principles, ensuring that execution behavior does not depend on secret inputs. However, validating the constant-time property of programs remains a major challenge in cryptography development. Formal method approaches to verify constant-time implementations rely on abstractions that often fail to capture real execution behavior, while timing-based measurement techniques are highly sensitive to noise from other programs and even hardware environments. In this work, we propose a novel approach for verifying constant-time programs based on dynamic analysis of low-level execution traces. Our method measures instruction sequences across multiple input values for any given binary and targeted function. Any variations in the instruction mix distribution for any given pair of traces indicate a deviation from the constant-time principle and behavior. We developed an open-source tool called DALC-CT, for the constant-time verification of programs using this approach. We evaluated it on a set of well-known constant-time and non-constant-time examples, achieving a perfect detection of issues. Our results demonstrate that analyzing the logical execution of programs via instruction trace comparisons provides a lightweight and reliable way to verify the constant-time property of programs.

</details>


### [139] [From Public-Key Linting to Operational Post-Quantum X.509 Assurance for ML-KEM and ML-DSA: Registry-Driven Policy, Mutation-Based Evaluation, and Import Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17003)
*José Luis Delgado Jiménez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出针对ML-KEM和ML-DSA在X.509环境中的工作流程中心化保障框架，通过严格检测机制确保后量子密码证书在签发前和私钥导入时的合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然ML-KEM和ML-DSA的FIPS和PKIX标准已确定规范底线，但在后量子X.509的实际操作中，仍需对证书配置文件语义、SubjectPublicKeyInfo表示和私钥容器导入进行可追溯检查以确保操作保障。

Method: 开发基于工作流程的保障框架，将17项最终标准要求转化为保障注册表，按所有者、阶段、检测器类型、规范强度和模式特定操作索引；分为三个操作门控包；涵盖证书/配置文件、SPKI/公钥和私钥容器/导入三个层面；通过冻结突变基准语料库进行评估。

Result: 在48个样本（21个有效，27个无效）中，框架在严格和可部署模式下均检测到所有预期无效案例，零误报。严格模式阻止所有17项活动要求；可部署模式保持相同检测覆盖率，仅将一项ML-KEM规范性条件从阻止降级为警告。私钥导入层面覆盖所有7项活动要求，检测到7/7预期无效案例。与JZLint基线相比，在证书子集上，本地框架达到10/10预期无效检测，无有效证书被错误拒绝。

Conclusion: 该框架支持可操作的X.509保障工作流程，适用于CA预签发和私钥导入场景，扩展了先前后量子公钥linting工作，证明解析接受度和策略合规性存在实质性差异。

Abstract: Final FIPS and PKIX standards for ML-KEM and ML-DSA fix the normative floor, but operational assurance in post-quantum X.509 still depends on accountable checks across certificate-profile semantics, SubjectPublicKeyInfo representation, and private-key-container import. We present a workflow-centric assurance framework for ML-KEM and ML-DSA in the narrow executable profile pkix-core. The framework reifies 17 final-standards requirements into an assurance registry indexed by owner, stage, detector kind, normative strength, and mode-specific action; groups them into three operator gate packs; spans certificate/profile, SPKI/public-key, and private-key-container/import surfaces; and evaluates them through a frozen mutation-based corpus with bounded public-appendix and cross-tool supporting evidence.
  Across a controlled corpus of 48 artifacts (21 valid, 27 invalid), the artifact detects all expected invalid cases in both strict and deployable modes with zero false positives. Strict blocks all 17 active requirements; deployable preserves the same detection coverage while downgrading exactly one exercised ML-KEM canonicality condition from block to warning. On the importer-owned private-key surface, all 7 active requirements are covered, with 7/7 expected invalid detections and no open detector gaps. On a comparable certificate subset, a frozen JZLint baseline meets 5/10 expected invalid detections and fatally rejects 3 valid ML-KEM certificates, whereas the local artifact meets 10/10 with no fatal valid rejections. A bounded public appendix and a cross-tool matrix further show that parse acceptance and policy conformance diverge materially. Overall, the results support an operational X.509 assurance workflow for CA pre-issuance and private-key import that extends prior PQ public-key linting work.

</details>


### [140] [HarmChip: Evaluating Hardware Security Centric LLM Safety via Jailbreak Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17093)
*Zeng Wang,Minghao Shao,Weimin Fu,Prithwish Basu Roy,Xiaolong Guo,Ramesh Karri,Muhammad Shafique,Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了HarmChip基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在硬件安全领域的越狱漏洞，发现现有模型存在对齐悖论：拒绝合法安全查询却接受语义伪装的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型集成到EDA工作流带来了强大的RTL生成、验证和设计优化能力，但也引发了关键的安全问题。恶意LLM输出可能导致硬件级威胁（如硬件木马插入、侧信道泄漏、知识产权盗窃），这些威胁一旦制成硅片就不可逆转。现有安全机制针对通用危害训练，无法检测嵌入在合法工程语言中的对抗意图，且缺乏评估LLM对此类领域特定威胁脆弱性的基准。

Method: 提出了HarmChip基准测试，涵盖16个硬件安全领域、120种威胁和360个提示，分为两个难度级别。该基准用于评估最先进LLM的越狱敏感性，特别关注语义伪装的攻击方式。

Result: 评估发现存在对齐悖论：LLM拒绝合法的安全查询，却遵守语义伪装的攻击。这暴露了安全护栏的盲点，突显了需要领域感知的安全对齐。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在硬件安全领域存在显著的安全漏洞，现有通用安全机制无法有效检测领域特定的语义伪装攻击。需要开发领域感知的安全对齐方法来应对硬件设计自动化中的安全威胁。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into electronic design automation (EDA) workflows has introduced powerful capabilities for RTL generation, verification, and design optimization, but also raises critical security concerns. Malicious LLM outputs in this domain pose hardware-level threats, including hardware Trojan insertion, side-channel leakage, and intellectual property theft, that are irreversible once fabricated into silicon. Such requests often exploit semantic disguise, embedding adversarial intent within legitimate engineering language that existing safety mechanisms, trained on general-purpose hazards, fail to detect. No benchmark exists to evaluate LLM vulnerability to such domain-specific threats. We present the HarmChip benchmark to assess jailbreak susceptibility in hardware security, spanning 16 hardware security domains, 120 threats, and 360 prompts at two difficulty levels. Evaluation of state-of-the-art LLMs reveals an alignment paradox: They refuse legitimate security queries while complying with semantically disguised attacks, exposing blind spots in safety guardrails and underscoring the need for domain-aware safety alignment.

</details>


### [141] [CASCADE: A Cascaded Hybrid Defense Architecture for Prompt Injection Detection in MCP-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17125)
*İpek Abasıkeleş Turgut,Edip Gümüş*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CASCADE是一个针对MCP系统的三层级联防御架构，通过本地化操作检测工具投毒和提示注入攻击，在5000样本数据集上达到95.85%精确率和74.59% F1分数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP（模型上下文协议）作为LLM应用中定义和调用外部工具的标准，其多层架构引入了新的攻击面（如工具投毒）和传统提示注入攻击。现有防御系统存在高误报率、API依赖或白盒访问要求等局限性，需要一种更有效的本地化防御方案。

Method: 提出CASCADE三层级联防御架构：第一层使用正则表达式、短语加权和熵分析进行快速预过滤；第二层通过BGE嵌入进行语义分析，配备Ollama Llama3回退机制；第三层应用基于模式的输出过滤。整个系统完全本地运行，无需外部API调用。

Result: 在5000个样本数据集上评估，获得95.85%精确率、6.06%误报率、61.05%召回率和74.59% F1分数。对31种攻击类型（分为6个层级）的分析显示：数据窃取检测率91.5%，提示注入检测率84.2%，语义攻击检测率52.5%，工具投毒检测率59.9%。

Conclusion: CASCADE为MCP系统提供了一种有效的本地化防御方案，相比现有解决方案具有无需外部API调用的优势。虽然在语义攻击和工具投毒检测方面仍有改进空间，但整体表现出良好的防御能力。

Abstract: Model Context Protocol (MCP) is a rapidly adopted standard for defining and invoking external tools in LLM applications. The multi-layered architecture of MCP introduces new attack surfaces such as tool poisoning, in addition to traditional prompt injection. Existing defense systems suffer from limitations including high false positive rates, API dependency, or white-box access requirements. In this study, we propose CASCADE, a three-tiered cascaded defense architecture for MCP-based systems: (i) Layer 1 performs fast pre-filtering using regex, phrase weighting, and entropy analysis; (ii) Layer 2 conducts semantic analysis via BGE embedding with an Ollama Llama3 fallback mechanism; (iii) Layer 3 applies pattern-based output filtering. Evaluation on a dataset of 5,000 samples yielded 95.85% precision, 6.06% false positive rate, 61.05% recall, and 74.59% F1-score. Analysis across 31 attack types categorized into 6 tiers revealed high detection rates for data exfiltration (91.5%) and prompt injection (84.2%), while semantic attack (52.5%) and tool poisoning (59.9%) categories showed potential for improvement. A key advantage of CASCADE over existing solutions is its fully local operation, requiring no external API calls

</details>


### [142] [Decentralised Trust and Security Mechanisms for IoT Networks at the Edge: A Comprehensive Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17179)
*Khandoker Ashik Uz Zaman,Mahdi H. Miraz,Mohammed N. M. Ali*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 综述分析了物联网与边缘计算融合中的去中心化信任与安全机制，评估了联邦学习、零信任架构、轻量级区块链等技术在资源受限设备上的应用效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网与边缘计算的融合增加了对去中心化信任和安全机制的需求，这些机制需要在异构且资源受限的设备上运行，以替代集中式控制方案

Method: 分析了30项最新研究，比较了去中心化架构在建立信任、支持安全通信和实现入侵与异常检测方面的表现，评估了DFGL-LZTA、SecFedDNN、COSIER等框架

Result: 去中心化设计增强了隐私保护，减少了单点故障，改善了自适应威胁响应能力，但在可扩展性、效率和互操作性方面仍面临挑战

Conclusion: 研究确定了构建安全、弹性的信任感知物联网边缘生态系统的关键考虑因素和未来研究方向

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: The proliferation of the amalgamation of IoT and edge computing has increased the demand for decentralised trust and security mechanisms capable of operating across heterogeneous and resource-limited devices. Approaches such as federated learning, Zero Trust architectures, lightweight blockchain and distributed neural models offer alternatives to centralised control. OBJECTIVES: This review examines various state-of-the-art decentralised mechanisms and evaluates their effectiveness in terms of securing IoT networks at the edge. METHODS: Thirty recent studies were analysed to compare how decentralised architectures establish trust, support secure communication and enable intrusion and anomaly detection. Frameworks, such as DFGL-LZTA, SecFedDNN and COSIER were assessed. RESULTS: Decentralised designs enhance privacy, reduce single points of failure and improve adaptive threat response, though challenges remain in scalability, efficiency and interoperability. CONCLUSION: The study identifies key considerations and future research needs for building secure and resilient trust-aware IoT edge ecosystems.

</details>


### [143] [Breaking Euston: Recovering Private Inputs from Secure Inference by Exploiting Subspace Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17238)
*Jiaqi Zhao,Fengwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Euston框架的SVD矩阵传输协议存在隐私风险，攻击者可通过子空间泄漏恢复私有样本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Euston框架中基于奇异值分解的矩阵传输协议存在根本性隐私缺陷，该协议虽然声称能减少通信带宽，但实际上会泄漏随机掩码的子空间信息

Method: 通过分析Euston的SVD矩阵传输协议，发现随机掩码的子空间泄漏问题，设计恢复攻击方法，并在图像和语言数据集上进行实验验证

Result: 攻击能够有效恢复私有样本，证明了协议设计存在根本性隐私风险，模型所有者可以轻易获取用户隐私数据

Conclusion: Euston框架的SVD矩阵传输协议存在严重隐私漏洞，需要重新设计安全机制或采用更安全的替代方案

Abstract: In the 47th IEEE Symposium on Security and Privacy (IEEE S&P 2026), Gao et al. proposed an efficient and user-friendly secure transformer inference framework, namely Euston. In Euston, a singular value decomposition-based matrix transmission protocol is designed to efficiently transmit input matrices, reducing communication bandwidth by approximately 2.8 times. In this manuscript, we show that this transmission protocol introduces subspace leakage of random masks, enabling the model owner to recover private samples easily. We further validate the effectiveness of the recovery attack through simple experiments on image and language datasets, highlighting a fundamental privacy risk of the protocol design.

</details>


### [144] [A Unified Compliance Aggregator Framework for Automated Multi-Tool Security Assessment of Linux Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17256)
*Sheldon Paul,Izzat Alsmadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一合规聚合器(UCA)框架，整合多个开源安全工具输出为单一综合安全评分，通过归一化和加权聚合方法评估系统安全态势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算系统安全评估需要多种专用工具，这些工具关注不同方面（配置合规性、文件完整性、漏洞暴露等），其输出难以统一解释。现有工具输出异构且难以综合评估整体安全态势。

Method: 提出UCA框架，整合Lynis、OpenSCAP（STIG和CIS配置文件）、AIDE、Tripwire和Nmap NSE等开源安全工具。采用归一化过程将异构输出转换为0-100统一尺度，然后进行加权聚合。针对文件完整性测量，引入对数评分模型以改进先前线性方法的局限性。

Result: 在Ubuntu 22.04上对不同加固级别和环境进行实验，结果显示随着系统加固，综合评分持续改善。同时揭示了合规性工具和文件完整性工具之间的对比行为。通过基础Web服务器和DVWA系统两个案例研究展示了框架的实际应用场景。

Conclusion: UCA框架成功整合多个安全工具输出为单一综合评分，提供更全面的系统安全态势评估。对数评分模型改进了文件完整性测量，实验验证了框架的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Assessing the security posture of modern computing systems typically requires the use of multiple specialized tools. These tools focus on different aspects such as configuration compliance, file integrity, and vulnerability exposure, and their outputs are often difficult to interpret collectively. This paper introduces the Unified Compliance Aggregator (UCA), a framework that integrates several open-source security tools into a single composite score representing overall system security. The proposed framework combines outputs from Lynis, OpenSCAP (STIG and CIS profiles), AIDE, Tripwire, and Nmap NSE. A normalization process converts heterogeneous outputs into a consistent 0 to 100 scale, followed by weighted aggregation. We also introduce a logarithmic scoring model for file integrity measurements to address limitations observed in prior linear approaches. Experiments were conducted on Ubuntu 22.04 across different hardening levels and environments. Results show consistent improvement in composite scores as systems are hardened, while also revealing contrasting behavior between compliance and file integrity tools. Two case studies, a basic web server and a DVWA-based system illustrate how the framework can be applied in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [145] [SDLLMFuzz: Dynamic-static LLM-assisted greybox fuzzing for structured input programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17750)
*Yihao Zou,Tianming Zheng,Futai Zou,Yue Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SDLLMFuzz：一种结合动态-静态LLM辅助的灰盒模糊测试框架，针对结构化输入程序，通过LLM生成结构感知种子和静态崩溃分析形成统一反馈循环，显著提升漏洞发现效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灰盒模糊测试对结构化输入程序效果有限，因为严格的语法约束和有限的语义意识导致难以生成有效输入和探索深层执行路径。现有LLM方法主要关注种子生成，忽视了运行时反馈的有效利用。

Method: 提出SDLLMFuzz框架，集成基于LLM的结构感知种子生成与静态崩溃分析，形成统一反馈循环。利用LLM生成语法有效且语义多样的输入，同时从崩溃工件（核心转储、执行轨迹）中提取丰富语义信息来指导后续输入生成。

Result: 在Magma基准测试中评估多个结构化输入程序（libxml2、libpng、libsndfile）。实验结果显示，SDLLMFuzz在漏洞发现和发现时间方面显著优于传统灰盒模糊测试器和LLM辅助基线方法。

Conclusion: 将语义输入生成与反馈驱动优化相结合是提高结构化输入程序模糊测试性能的有效方向，动态-静态反馈机制能更高效地探索复杂程序行为。

Abstract: Fuzzing has become a widely adopted technique for vulnerability discovery, yet it remains ineffective for structured-input programs due to strict syntactic constraints and limited semantic awareness. Traditional greybox fuzzers rely on mutation-based strategies and coarse-grained coverage feedback, which often fail to generate valid inputs and explore deep execution paths. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in improving input generation, but existing approaches primarily focus on seed generation and largely overlook the effective use of runtime feedback. In this paper, we propose SDLLMFuzz, a dynamic-static LLM-assisted greybox fuzzing framework for structured-input programs. Our approach integrates LLM-based structure-aware seed generation with static crash analysis, forming a unified feedback loop that iteratively refines test inputs. Specifically, we leverage LLMs to generate syntactically valid and semantically diverse inputs, while extracting rich semantic information from crash artifacts (e.g., core dumps and execution traces) to guide subsequent input generation. This dynamic-static feedback mechanism enables more efficient exploration of complex program behaviors. We evaluate SDLLMFuzz on the Magma benchmark across multiple structured-input programs, including libxml2, libpng, and libsndfile. Experimental results show that SDLLMFuzz significantly outperforms traditional greybox fuzzers and LLM-assisted baselines in terms of bug discovery and time-to-bug. These results demonstrate that combining semantic input generation with feedback-driven refinement is an effective direction for improving fuzzing performance on structured-input programs.

</details>


### [146] [A Quasi-Experimental Developer Study of Security Training in LLM-Assisted Web Application Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17763)
*Mohammed Kharma,Ahmed Sabbah,Radi Jarrar,Samer Zain,Mohammad Alkhanafseh,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 层式安全训练包与LLM辅助Java Spring Boot后端开发中安全质量改善的关联性研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是评估在LLM辅助的后端开发中，专门的安全训练是否能够改善代码安全质量，而不需要修改模型本身。当前LLM辅助开发日益普及，但安全风险随之增加，需要验证训练干预的有效性。

Method: 采用准实验混合设计：1) 受试者内设计（训练前vs训练后比较）；2) 探索性受试者间设计（专业技能因素）。12名开发者在统一界面、固定模型配置、平衡任务集和共享起始项目下完成匹配运行。安全结果通过第一和第二作者对提交仓库的独立人工验证评估。主要终点为严重性加权验证弱点分数。

Result: 训练后条件显示显著配对减少（Wilcoxon符号秩检验p=0.0059）。总体验证弱点从162降至111（减少31.5%），严重性加权负担从432降至267（减少38.2%），关键发现从24降至5（减少79.2%）。最大改善在授权和对象访问（53.3%）以及认证、凭证策略和恢复弱点（44.7%）。会话和浏览器信任边界问题变化最小，敏感数据和加密弱点仅边际改善。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，训练后运行减少了LLM辅助后端开发中的验证安全负担，且无需修改模型。但结果不支持替代安全默认设置、静态分析、专家审查或操作强化。训练干预对特定安全领域（如授权、认证）效果显著，但对其他领域（如会话管理、加密）改善有限。

Abstract: This paper presents a controlled quasi-experimental developer study examining whether a layer-based security training package is associated with improved security quality in LLM-assisted implementation of an identity-centric Java Spring Boot backend. The study uses a mixed design with a within-subject pre-training versus post-training comparison and an exploratory between-subject expertise factor. Twelve developers completed matched runs under a common interface, fixed model configuration, counterbalanced task sets, and a shared starter project. Security outcomes were assessed via independent manual validation of submitted repositories by the first and second authors. The primary participant-level endpoint was a severity-weighted validated-weakness score. The post-training condition showed a significant paired reduction under an exact Wilcoxon signed-rank test ($p = 0.0059$). In aggregate, validated weaknesses decreased from 162 to 111 (31.5\%), the severity-weighted burden decreased from 432 to 267 (38.2\%), and critical findings decreased from 24 to 5 (79.2\%). The largest reductions were in authorization and object access (53.3\%) and in authentication, credential policy, and recovery weaknesses (44.7\%). Session and browser trust-boundary issues showed minimal change, while sensitive-data and cryptographic weaknesses showed only marginal improvement.
  These results suggest that, under the tested conditions, post-training runs reduce validated security burden in LLM-assisted backend development without modifying the model. They do not support replacing secure defaults, static analysis, expert review, or operational hardening.

</details>


### [147] [SoK: Analysis of Privacy Risks and Mitigation in Online Propaganda Detection through the PROMPT Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17788)
*Dhiman Goswami,Al Nahian Bin Emran,Md Hasan Ullah Sadi,Sanchari Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出PROMPT框架，用于分析在线宣传检测系统中的隐私风险，并通过量化权衡函数平衡隐私保护、性能损失和部署成本，同时引入合规性评分评估现有方法对GDPR等法规的遵循程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线宣传检测系统在数据收集、特征提取和模型推理等多个阶段存在可测量的隐私风险。现有研究缺乏对隐私风险的系统性分析，以及如何在保持检测性能的同时满足法规要求的实用框架。

Method: 1. 对162篇同行评审研究进行结构化分析；2. 提出PROMPT框架，通过映射函数M: R→S将风险与缓解策略关联，使用权衡函数α·PrivacyGain(s_j) - β·PerfLoss(s_j) - γ·Cost(s_j)优化策略选择；3. 引入合规性评分量化现有方法对GDPR、CCPA等法规的遵循程度；4. 在合成扰动下对基于Transformer的编码器和解码器进行微调实验。

Result: 1. 许多广泛使用的检测流程在元数据处理和用户级聚合方面仍不符合法规要求；2. 实验显示隐私-性能的单调权衡关系：在q=0.05时性能下降1-2% F1，在q=0.20时下降达到13-14%；3. 建立了宣传检测中隐私成本的量化基准。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了形式化的风险-防御映射框架、面向合规性的审计指标以及隐私-性能权衡的实验证据，为构建符合法规要求且具有隐私意识的检测系统奠定了技术基础。

Abstract: Online propaganda detection pipelines expose measurable privacy risks at multiple stages including data collection, feature extraction, and model inference. We conduct a structured analysis of $162$ peer-reviewed studies and formalize the problem using the Propaganda Risk Online Mitigation and Privacy-preserving Tactics (PROMPT) framework. PROMPT models risks $R$ and mitigation strategies $S$ through a mapping $M: R\to S$ guided by a utility function $α\cdot \mathrm{PrivacyGain}(s_j) - β\cdot \mathrm{PerfLoss}(s_j) - γ\cdot \mathrm{Cost}(s_j)$, with tunable $(α,β,γ)$ enabling stakeholders to balance privacy, accuracy, and deployment costs. To assess practical adoption, we introduce a compliance score that quantifies the alignment of existing methods with GDPR, CCPA etc. requirements. Our evaluation shows that many widely used pipelines remain non-compliant, particularly in metadata handling and user-level aggregation. We further present empirical fine-tuning experiments on transformer-based encoders and decoders under synthetic perturbation, demonstrating a monotonic privacy-utility trade-off: with $q = 0.05$ performance decreased by 1-2% F$_1$, while at $q = 0.20$ the reduction reached 13-14%. These results establish quantitative baselines for privacy costs in propaganda detection. Our contributions include a formal risk-to-defense mapping, a compliance-oriented auditing metric, and experimental evidence of privacy-performance trade-offs, providing a technical foundation for building regulation-compliant and privacy-aware detection systems.

</details>


### [148] [Understanding Secret Leakage Risks in Code LLMs: A Tokenization Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17814)
*Meifang Chen,Zhe Yang,Huang Nianchen,Yizhan Huang,Yichen Li,Zihan Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现BPE分词导致代码大语言模型意外记忆代码密钥，称为"乱码偏差"，表现为高字符熵但低令牌熵的密钥最易被记忆


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代码助手快速发展，但代码大语言模型因记忆现象会无意泄露敏感代码密钥，需要研究其根本原因

Method: 分析BPE分词对密钥记忆的影响，识别"乱码偏差"现象，通过数值数据支持偏差主张，研究训练数据与密钥数据的令牌分布偏移

Result: 发现某些高字符熵但低令牌熵的密钥最易被CLLMs记忆，根源在于训练数据与密钥数据的令牌分布偏移，并探讨了"更大词汇表"趋势下的表现

Conclusion: 讨论了潜在缓解策略和当前分词器设计的更广泛影响，为改进AI代码助手安全性提供方向

Abstract: Code secrets are sensitive assets for software developers, and their leakage poses significant cybersecurity risks. While the rapid development of AI code assistants powered by Code Large Language Models (CLLMs), CLLMs are shown to inadvertently leak such secrets due to a notorious memorization phenomenon. This study first reveals that Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) tokenization leads to unexpected behavior of secret memorization, which we term as \textit{gibberish bias}. Specifically, we identified that some secrets are among the easiest for CLLMs to memorize. These secrets yield high character-level entropy, but low token-level entropy. Then, this paper supports the biased claim with numerical data. We identified that the roots of the bias are the token distribution shift between the CLLM training data and the secret data. We further discuss how gibberish bias manifests under the ``larger vocabulary'' trend. To conclude the paper, we discuss potential mitigation strategies and the broader implications on current tokenizer design.

</details>


### [149] [Privacy-Preserving Product-Quantized Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search Framework for Large-scale Datasets via A Hybrid of Fully Homomorphic Encryption and Trusted Execution Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17816)
*Shozo Saeki,Minoru Kawahara,Hirohisa Aman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PPPQ-ANN：基于FHE和TEE混合的隐私保护乘积量化近似最近邻框架，在百万级数据集上实现2小时内数据库生成和50+ QPS搜索性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PP-ANN方法（基于TEE或FHE）在安全性和性能上存在不足，且主要关注搜索过程而非数据库生成。向量相似性搜索中的丰富信息会带来嵌入反转和成员攻击等安全风险，需要更实用的隐私保护方案。

Method: 提出PPPQ-ANN框架：1）基于FHE和TEE混合的多层安全结构保护向量；2）结合乘积量化（PQ）和优化数据打包最小化FHE密文计算；3）同时保护数据库生成和搜索过程。

Result: 在百万级数据集上，PPPQ-ANN实现数据库生成时间小于2小时，顺序搜索吞吐量超过50 QPS，在保持隐私的同时达到实用性能水平。

Conclusion: PPPQ-ANN通过FHE和TEE的混合使用优化了安全性与性能的权衡，为LLM/VLM应用中的隐私保护近似最近邻搜索提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: A nearest-neighbor framework is a fundamental tool for various applications involving Large Language Models (LLMs) and Visual Language Models (VLMs). Vectors used for nearest-neighbor searches have richer information for similarity searches. This information leads to security risks, such as embedding inversion and membership attacks. Therefore, Privacy-Preserving Approximate Nearest-Neighbor (PP-ANN) approaches are necessary for highly confidential data. However, conventional PP-ANN approaches based on a Trusted Execution Environment (TEE) or Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) do not achieve practical security or performance. Additionally, conventional approaches focus on the search process rather than database generation for nearest-neighbor. To address these issues, we propose a Privacy-Preserving Product-Quantization Approximate Nearest Neighbor (PPPQ-ANN) framework. PPPQ-ANN provides a multi-layered security structure for vectors based on a hybrid of FHE and TEE. Additionally, PPPQ-ANN minimizes FHE ciphertext computations by combining Product-Quantization (PQ) with optimized data packing. We demonstrate the performance of PPPQ-ANN on million-scale datasets. As a result, PPPQ-ANN achieves database generation in less than 2 hours and more than 50 QPS in a sequential search while preserving privacy. Therefore, PPPQ-ANN optimizes the trade-off between security and performance by utilizing a hybrid of FHE and TEE, achieving practical performance while preserving privacy.

</details>


### [150] [Audit-or-Cast: Enforcing Honest Elections with Privacy-Preserving Public Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18163)
*Aman Rojjha,Gaurang Tandon,Varul Srivastava,Kannan Srinathan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ACE投票协议通过公开可验证但隐藏计票结果的聚合机制和"审计或投票"挑战，在无需可信客户端的情况下同时实现端到端可验证性、强隐私保护和抗胁迫性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子投票系统在公开可验证性和选民隐私/抗胁迫性之间存在矛盾：要么公开投票分布破坏隐私，要么依赖可信客户端或可转移收据降低可信度。需要一种能同时满足公开审计和强隐私保证的解决方案。

Method: ACE协议结合了公开可验证但隐藏计票结果的聚合机制，以及"审计或投票"挑战来强制实现"按意图投票"，即使在不信任客户端的情况下。通过计票方重随机化消除选民与公开记录之间的持久链接，在至少有一个诚实计票方时实现信息论上的无收据性。

Result: ACE协议被形式化证明能同时实现端到端可验证性、公开隐藏计票结果和强无收据性，且无需可信客户端假设。

Conclusion: ACE协议成功调和了电子投票系统中公开可验证性与强隐私保护之间的矛盾，通过创新的密码学设计在现实部署中同时满足审计需求和隐私要求。

Abstract: Electronic voting systems must balance public verifiability with voter privacy and coercion resistance. Existing cryptographic protocols typically achieve end-to-end verifiability by revealing vote distributions, relying on trusted clients, or enabling transferable receipts - design choices that often compromise trust or privacy in real-world deployments.
  We present ACE, a voting protocol that reconciles public auditability with strong privacy guarantees. The protocol combines a publicly verifiable, tally-hiding aggregation mechanism with an Audit-or-Cast challenge that enforces cast-as-intended even under untrusted client assumptions. Tallier-side re-randomization eliminates persistent links between voters and public records, yielding information-theoretic receipt-freeness assuming at least one honest tallier.
  We formalize the security of ACE and show that it simultaneously achieves end-to-end verifiability, publicly tally-hiding results, and strong receipt-freeness without trusted clients.

</details>


### [151] [Committed SAE-Feature Traces for Audited-Session Substitution Detection in Hosted LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18179)
*Ziyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于承诺-打开协议的LLM服务验证方案，通过Merkle树承诺稀疏自编码器特征轨迹，防止服务商用廉价模型替代广告模型，解决了SVIP等方案中的并行服务侧信道漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管LLM服务商存在"静默替换"动机：宣传强大模型却实际提供廉价回复。现有SVIP等方案存在并行服务侧信道漏洞，不诚实服务商可将验证者的探测请求路由到广告模型，而普通用户则使用替代模型。

Method: 提出承诺-打开协议：服务商在公开探测层通过Merkle树承诺其服务输出的稀疏自编码器特征轨迹草图；验证者随机打开位置，使用公开命名电路探测库进行评分，采用固定阈值联合一致性z-score规则决策。在三个骨干模型上实例化协议。

Result: 对17种攻击者（包括同族提升、跨族替代和秩≤128的自适应LoRA）均能拒绝，共享尺度稳定阈值；相同攻击者均能规避匹配的SVIP风格并行服务基线。白盒端到端攻击和特征伪造攻击均无法突破安全边界。承诺开销在批次32时仅增加≤2.1%的前向时钟时间。

Conclusion: 提出的承诺-打开协议有效关闭了LLM服务验证中的并行服务侧信道漏洞，提供可扩展的验证方案，能够检测多种攻击类型，同时保持较低的计算开销。
