Abstract: Hosted-LLM providers have a silent-substitution incentive: advertise a stronger model while serving cheaper replies. Probe-after-return schemes such as SVIP leave a parallel-serve side-channel, since a dishonest provider can route the verifier's probe to the advertised model while serving ordinary users from a substitute. We propose a commit-open protocol that closes this gap. Before any opening request, the provider commits via a Merkle tree to a per-position sparse-autoencoder (SAE) feature-trace sketch of its served output at a published probe layer. A verifier opens random positions, scores them against a public named-circuit probe library calibrated with cross-backend noise, and decides with a fixed-threshold joint-consistency z-score rule. We instantiate the protocol on three backbones -- Qwen3-1.7B, Gemma-2-2B, and a 4.5x scale-up to Gemma-2-9B with a 131k-feature SAE. Of 17 attackers spanning same-family lifts, cross-family substitutes, and rank-<=128 adaptive LoRA, all are rejected at a shared, scale-stable threshold; the same attackers all evade a matched SVIP-style parallel-serve baseline. A white-box end-to-end attack that backpropagates through the frozen SAE encoder does not close the margin, and a feature-forgery attacker that never runs M_hon is bounded in closed form by an intrinsic-dimension argument. Commitment adds <=2.1% to forward-only wall-clock at batch 32.

</details>


### [152] [Subcodes of Lambda-Gabidulin Codes for Compact-Ciphertext Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18282)
*Freddy Lendé Metouké,Hervé Talé Kalachi,Hermann Tchatchiem Kamche,Ousmane Ndiaye,Sélestin Ndjeya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究λ-Gabidulin码的子码及其在紧凑密文密码系统中的应用，提出基于生成矩阵的随机子码构造，并设计MinRank设置下的McEliece类和Niederreiter类加密方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究λ-Gabidulin码的子码结构，探索其在紧凑密文密码系统中的应用潜力，旨在设计具有最小密文尺寸的加密方案。

Method: 1. 分析λ-Gabidulin码的子空间子码和广义子空间子码，通过坐标缩放将其与经典Gabidulin码子码关联；2. 当扩展度等于码长时，用线性化多项式表征Gabidulin子空间子码；3. 通过稳定子和零化子代数研究这些子码在基域上的矩阵像；4. 提出基于生成矩阵的随机子码构造以避免代数不变量；5. 在MinRank设置下设计McEliece类和Niederreiter类加密方案。

Result: 1. 建立了λ-Gabidulin码子码与经典Gabidulin码子码的对应关系，获得了基数界限和结构性质；2. 给出了Gabidulin子空间子码的显式编码和维度描述；3. 证明了子空间限制可能保持非平凡代数不变量；4. 提出的LGS-Niederreiter实例在128、192和256比特安全级别下，在比较的方案中实现了最小的密文尺寸，同时保持了有竞争力的公钥尺寸。

Conclusion: λ-Gabidulin码的子码为设计紧凑密文密码系统提供了有前景的构造基础，其中经典Gabidulin码的随机子码在MinRank设置下能够实现最紧凑的密文，在安全性和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates subcodes of lambda-Gabidulin codes, viewed as rank-metric analogues of generalized Reed--Solomon codes, and their applications to compact-ciphertext cryptosystems. We first analyze subspace and generalized subspace subcodes of lambda-Gabidulin codes and relate them to corresponding subcodes of classical Gabidulin codes through coordinate-wise scaling. This relation yields cardinality bounds and structural properties for these families. When the extension degree equals the code length, we further characterize Gabidulin subspace subcodes in terms of linearized polynomials, which gives an explicit description of their encoding and dimension. We also study the matrix images of these subcodes over the base field through their stabilizer and annihilator algebras, showing that subspace restrictions may preserve nontrivial algebraic invariants despite the loss of extension-field linearity. Motivated by these results, we propose a generator-matrix-based construction of random subcodes designed to avoid such invariants. This construction is then used to design McEliece-like and Niederreiter-like encryption schemes in the MinRank setting. Among the parameter sets considered in this work, the most compact ciphertexts are obtained from random subcodes of classical Gabidulin codes. At the 128-, 192-, and 256-bit security levels, the resulting $\mathsf{LGS}$-Niederreiter instances achieve the smallest ciphertext sizes among the compared schemes, while maintaining competitive public-key sizes.

</details>


### [153] [Tight Auditing of Differential Privacy in MST and AIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18352)
*Georgi Ganev,Meenatchi Sundaram Muthu Selva Annamalai,Bogdan Kulynych*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于高斯差分隐私(GDP)的审计框架，用于严格评估MST和AIM等DP合成数据生成器的隐私保证，在强隐私机制下首次实现紧致审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的差分隐私合成数据生成器（如MST和AIM）被广泛使用，但严格审计其隐私保证仍然具有挑战性。需要开发能够通过完整假阳性/假阴性权衡来测量隐私的审计方法。

Method: 引入基于高斯差分隐私(GDP)的审计框架，通过完整的假阳性/假阴性权衡来测量隐私。该方法应用于MST和AIM在最坏情况设置下，在强隐私机制下提供首次紧致审计。

Result: 对于(ε,δ)=(1,10^{-2})的参数设置，获得μ_{emp}≈0.43与隐含μ=0.45的比较，显示出较小的理论与实践差距。代码已公开可用。

Conclusion: 提出的GDP审计框架能够对DP合成数据生成器进行紧致隐私审计，特别是在强隐私机制下，为评估隐私保证提供了有效工具。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Differentially Private (DP) synthetic data generators such as MST and AIM are widely used, yet tightly auditing their privacy guarantees remains challenging. We introduce a Gaussian Differential Privacy (GDP)-based auditing framework that measures privacy via the full false-positive/false-negative tradeoff. Applied to MST and AIM under worst-case settings, our method provides the first tight audits in the strong-privacy regime. For $(ε,δ)=(1,10^{-2})$, we obtain $μ_{emp}\approx0.43$ vs. implied $μ=0.45$, showing a small theory-practice gap.
  Our code is publicly available: https://github.com/sassoftware/dpmm.

</details>


### [154] [Capturing Monetarily Exploitable Vulnerability in Smart Contracts via Auditor Knowledge-Learning Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18395)
*Bowen Cai,Weiheng Bai,Hangyun Tang,Youshui Lu,Kangjie Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FAUDITOR是一种专门用于检测智能合约中货币可被利用漏洞(MEVuls)的模糊测试工具，通过整合金融相关接口、审计报告NLP分析和自学习机制，显著提升了检测效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约的复杂性带来了严重的安全挑战，特别是货币可被利用漏洞(MEVuls)。现有安全工具难以有效识别这些漏洞，常常产生过多警告而无法提供可操作的见解，导致区块链生态系统面临持续的金融攻击威胁。

Method: 首先形式化MEVuls定义，然后开发FAUDITOR专门模糊测试工具。关键创新包括：1) 利用智能合约金融相关接口直接暴露关键漏洞；2) 使用NLP分析审计报告提取攻击模式；3) 采用自学习机制基于先前模糊测试结果优化检测策略。

Result: FAUDITOR在评估中发现了220个零日MEVuls漏洞。相比现有模糊测试工具，FAUDITOR检测漏洞更快，实现了更好的指令覆盖率，显著提升了检测效率和准确性。

Conclusion: FAUDITOR通过专门针对MEVuls的检测方法，有效解决了智能合约中复杂金融漏洞的识别挑战。其整合金融接口、NLP分析和自学习机制的方法为智能合约安全工具开发提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Smart contracts extended blockchain functionality beyond simple transactions, powering complex applications like decentralized finance (DeFi). However, this complexity introduces serious security challenges, including price manipulation and inflation attacks. Despite the development of various security tools, the rapid rise in financially motivated exploits continues to pose a significant threat to the blockchain ecosystem. These financially motivated exploits often stem from Monetarily Exploitable Vulnerabilities (MEVuls), which refer to vulnerabilities arising from exploitable implementations in monetary transactions or value-transfer logic. Due to their complexity, intricate chains of function calls, multifaceted logic, and diverse manifestations across different smart contracts, MEVuls are particularly challenging for current security tools to identify. Instead of providing actionable insights, existing tools frequently generate excessive warnings that overwhelm developers without effectively mitigating risks. To address the challenge of recognizing MEVuls, we first formalize MEVuls based on common real-world financial exploits. Then, we introduce FAUDITOR, a specialized fuzzer designed to detect MEVuls in smart contracts. The key insight is that leveraging smart contracts' finance-related interfaces directly exposes critical vulnerabilities, making detection more targeted. We further integrate auditors' reports using NLP to extract valuable insights on exploitation patterns, enabling a more informed search strategy. Additionally, FAUDITOR employs a self-learning mechanism that refines its detection strategies over time, allowing it to improve based on prior fuzzing results. In our evaluation, FAUDITOR impressively reveals 220 zero-day MEVuls. Meanwhile, compared to existing fuzzers, FAUDITOR detects vulnerabilities faster and achieves better instruction coverage.

</details>


### [155] [Different Paths to Harmful Compliance: Behavioral Side Effects and Mechanistic Divergence Across LLM Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18510)
*Md Rysul Kabir,Zoran Tiganj*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究比较了三种使开源语言模型变得不安全的干预方法：有害监督微调、有害强化学习和拒绝抑制消除，发现尽管都能达到高有害合规率，但在能力、行为特征和内部失效模式上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解不同"越狱"方法对语言模型产生的不同影响。虽然多种干预都能使模型变得不安全，但不同方法可能导致模型在能力、行为特征和内部机制上产生本质差异，这对模型安全评估和修复具有重要意义。

Method: 研究方法包括：1）三种不安全干预：有害监督微调、有害强化学习与可验证奖励、拒绝抑制消除；2）行为评估：有害合规率、能力退化、结构化自我审计；3）机制分析：安全几何结构、策略行为重定向、分布式漂移；4）修复实验：针对性修复对不同越狱模型的效果。

Result: 主要结果：1）三种方法都能达到接近天花板的有害合规率；2）RLVR越狱模型能力退化最小，保留明确的危害识别能力，但会遵从有害请求；3）SFT越狱模型安全判断崩溃最严重，能力损失最大；4）消除方法在自我审计和安全支架响应上呈现家族依赖性；5）针对性修复对RLVR模型有效，对SFT模型无效。

Conclusion: 结论是不同越狱方法会产生截然不同的模型特性，尽管表面有害合规率相似。RLVR越狱模型与基础模型最为相似，保留了安全几何结构但重定向了策略行为；SFT导致广泛的分布式漂移；消除方法表现为局部拒绝特征删除。这对模型安全评估和修复策略有重要启示。

Abstract: Open-weight language models can be rendered unsafe through several distinct interventions, but the resulting models may differ substantially in capabilities, behavioral profile, and internal failure mode. We study behavioral and mechanistic properties of jailbroken models across three unsafe routes: harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT), harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and refusal-suppressing abliteration. All three routes achieve near-ceiling harmful compliance, but they diverge once we move beyond direct harmfulness. RLVR-jailbroken models show minimal degradation and preserve explicit harm recognition in a structured self-audit: they are able to identify harmful prompts and describe how a safe LLM should respond, yet they comply with the harmful request. With RLVR, harmful behavior is strongly suppressed by a reflective safety scaffold: when a harmful prompt is prepended with an instruction to reflect on safety standards, harmful behavior drops close to the baseline. Category-specific RLVR jailbreaks generalize broadly across harmfulness domains. Models jailbroken with SFT show the largest collapse in explicit safety judgments, the highest behavioral drift, and a substantial capability loss on standard benchmarks. Abliteration is family-dependent in both self-audit and response to a reflective safety scaffold. Mechanistic and repair analyses further separate the routes: abliteration is consistent with localized refusal-feature deletion, RLVR with preserved safety geometry but retargeted policy behavior, and SFT with broader distributed drift. Targeted repair partially recovers RLVR-jailbroken models, but has little effect on SFT-jailbroken models. Together, these results show that jailbreaks can produce vastly different properties despite similar harmfulness, with models jailbroken via RLVR showing remarkable similarity to the base model.

</details>


### [156] [Do Privacy Policies Match with the Logs? An Empirical Study of Privacy Disclosure in Android Application Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18552)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Love Jayesh Ahir,Ahmad Suleiman,Kundi Yao,Yiming Tang,Weiyi Shang,Daqing Hou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对1000个Android应用隐私政策中日志相关声明的实证研究，发现隐私政策对日志实践的描述不完整且模糊，与实际日志行为存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私政策本应告知用户软件系统如何收集和处理数据，但往往模糊或不完整。本研究旨在探索隐私政策中日志相关声明的模式，以及这些声明与Android应用实际日志中观察到的隐私披露之间的一致性。

Method: 分析了1000个跨多个类别的Android应用，生成了86,836,964条日志条目。通过实证研究方法，比较隐私政策中关于日志实践的声明与实际应用日志中的隐私披露情况。

Result: 1. 88.0%的应用提供隐私政策，但只有28.5%明确提及日志实践；2. 提及日志的应用中，27.7%的声明过于简单或模糊；3. 67.6%的应用在日志中泄露了隐私政策未提及的敏感信息；4. 仅4%的应用在声明内容与实际日志数据之间表现出一致性。

Conclusion: 当前隐私政策对日志实践的描述不完整且模糊，与实际日志行为存在显著差异，表明隐私政策在准确反映应用数据收集实践方面存在严重不足。

Abstract: Privacy policies are intended to inform users about how software systems collect and handle data, yet they often remain vague or incomplete. This paper presents an empirical study of patterns in log-related statements within privacy policies and their alignment with privacy disclosures observed in Android application logs. We analyzed 1,000 Android apps across multiple categories, generating 86,836,964 log entries. Our findings reveal that while most applications (88.0%) provide privacy policies, only 28.5% explicitly mention logging practices. Among those that reference logging, most clearly describe what information is logged; however, 27.7% of log-related statements remain overly simplistic or vague, offering limited insight into actual data collection. We further observed widespread privacy leakages in application logs, with 67.6% of apps leaking sensitive information not mentioned in their policies. Alarmingly, only 4% of applications demonstrated consistent alignment between declared policy contents and actual logged data. These findings highlight that current privacy policies provide incomplete or ambiguous descriptions of logging practices, which frequently do not align with actual logging behaviors.

</details>
